id="kb-main-content" class="col-span-12 @lg:col-span-8 text-copy-16 lg:text-copy-18 flex flex-col items-center justify-start gap-6 flex-initial [&amp;&gt;*:not([data-kb-media-breakout])]:w-full [&amp;&gt;*]:min-w-0 [&amp;_[data-slot=note]_p]:my-0 hover:[&amp;_[data-slot=note]_a]:no-underline [&amp;_code_p]:my-0 [&amp;_code_p]:contents [&amp;_ol]:p-0 [&amp;_ol]:pl-4 [&amp;_ul]:p-0 [&amp;_ul]:pl-4 [&amp;_ol]:list-decimal [&amp;_ul]:list-disc [&amp;_ol]:ml-0 [&amp;_ul]:ml-0 md:[&amp;_ol]:ml-1 md:[&amp;_ul]:ml-1 [&amp;_[class*=&quot;container&quot;]_p]:m-0"><p class="text-pretty break-words">AI agents that generate and run code need three things: durable orchestration that survives failures, isolated execution that protects your infrastructure, and observable model access you can swap without rewriting code. Vercel Workflows, Sandbox, and AI Gateway solve each of these. </p><p class="text-pretty break-words">This guide covers how to combine all three in a single Next.js app that generates code from a natural language prompt, writes its own tests, runs them in an isolated microVM, and retries automatically when tests fail.</p><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#overview" id="overview"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Overview</h2><p class="text-pretty break-words">In this guide, you&#x27;ll learn how to:</p><ul class="my-3 list-disc pl-6 [&amp;&gt;li::marker]:text-gray-500 !my-0"><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Define a durable, multi-step workflow using the <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">&quot;use workflow&quot;</code> and <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">&quot;use step&quot;</code> compiler directives</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Execute AI-generated code safely in a Sandbox microVM</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Route LLM calls through AI Gateway with a single API key</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Build a self-healing agentic loop that retries on failure</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Observe every step, LLM call, and execution result in the Vercel Dashboard</li></ul><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#how-it-works" id="how-it-works"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>How it works</h2><p class="text-pretty break-words">A user submits a coding task in plain English. A Workflow orchestrates the entire process: </p><ul class="my-3 list-disc pl-6 [&amp;&gt;li::marker]:text-gray-500 !my-0"><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">AI Gateway routes a request to Claude to generate code</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">A second AI Gateway call generates test cases</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Sandbox spins up an isolated microVM to run the code and tests.</li></ul><p class="text-pretty break-words">If the tests fail, the Workflow loops back to the generation step with the error context, and the cycle repeats up to three times. Every step is durable, observable, and automatic.</p><div data-kb-copy-code="User prompt

│

▼

POST /api/evaluate → start(evaluateCode, [prompt])

│

▼

evaluateCode() ← &quot;use workflow&quot;

│

├── generateCode() ← &quot;use step&quot; + AI Gateway

├── generateTests() ← &quot;use step&quot; + AI Gateway

├── executeInSandbox() ← &quot;use step&quot; + Sandbox

├── Tests pass?

│ ├── Yes → return results

│ └── No → loop back with error context (max 3 attempts)

│

▼

Results returned" data-kb-copy-language="markdown"><div class="rounded-md group/copy relative overflow-hidden border border-[var(--ds-gray-400)] rounded-b-md bg-[var(--ds-bfackground-100)] my-0 returned</span></div></div></code></pre></div></div><p class="text-pretty break-words">The three primitives each handle a distinct responsibility:</p><ul class="my-3 list-disc pl-6 [&amp;&gt;li::marker]:text-gray-500 !my-0"><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Workflows</span> manages orchestration, retries, and state. If a step crashes, it replays deterministically from the last completed step.</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Sandbox</span> handles execution. AI-generated code runs in an ephemeral Firecracker microVM with its own filesystem and network. Nothing touches your server.</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">AI Gateway</span> handles model access. One API key, any model, with spend tracking and observability built in.</li></ul><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#prerequisites" id="prerequisites"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Prerequisites</h2><p class="text-pretty break-words">Before you begin, make sure you have:</p><ul class="my-3 list-disc pl-6 [&amp;&gt;li::marker]:text-gray-500 !my-0"><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">A <a href="https://vercel.com/signup" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-zone="null" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-[var(--geist-link-color)] [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:underline [&amp;_u]:no-underline underline decoration-1 underline-offset-2 text-inherit! hover:decoration-1! hover:text-(--geist-link-color)! focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2">Vercel account</a></li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">A Vercel project linked to your local repository. AI Gateway and Sandbox both authenticate automatically via OIDC tokens when deployed on Vercel. For local development, <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">vercel env pull</code> pulls the token into your <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">.env.local</code>.</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Basic knowledge of TypeScript and Next.js</li><li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">The <a href="https://vercel.com/docs/cli" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-zone="null" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-[var(--geist-link-color)] [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:underline [&amp;_u]:no-underline underline decoration-1 underline-offset-2 text-inherit! hover:decoration-1! hover:text-(--geist-link-color)! focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2">Vercel CLI</a> installed</li></ul><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#1.-set-up-the-project" id="1.-set-up-the-project"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>1. Define the workflow</h2><p class="text-pretty break-words">Create <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">workflows/evaluate-code.ts</code>. This file is the orchestrator: it coordinates the steps, handles the retry loop, and passes error context between iterations.</p><div data-kb-copy-code="import { generateText } from &quot;ai&quot;;

import { gateway } from &quot;@ai-sdk/gateway&quot;;

import { Sandbox } from &quot;@vercel/sandbox&quot;;


// LLMs often wrap output in markdown code fences even when told not to.

// Strip them so the raw code can be written directly to the sandbox.

function stripCodeFences(text: string): string {

return text.replace(/^```[\w]*\n?/gm, &quot;&quot;).replace(/```\s*$/gm, &quot;&quot;).trim();

}


export async function evaluateCode(prompt: string) {

&quot;use workflow&quot;;


const maxIterations = 3;

let iteration = 0;

let lastError: string | null = null;


while (iteration &lt; maxIterations) {

iteration++;


const code = await generateCode(prompt, lastError);

const tests = await generateTests(prompt, code);

const result = await executeInSandbox(code, tests);


if (result.passed) {

return {

success: true,

code,

tests,

output: result.output,

iterations: iteration,

};

}


lastError = result.error;

}


return {

success: false,

error: `Failed after ${maxIterations} iterations. Last error: ${lastError}`,

iterations: maxIterations,

};

}" data-kb-copy-language="typescript"><div class="rounded-md group/copy relative overflow-hidden border border-[var(--ds-gray-400)] rounded-b-md bg-[var(--ds-bfackground-100)] my-0 </span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L38" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword">return</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L39" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> success</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token boolean">false</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L40" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> error</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token template-string template-punctuation string">`</span><span class="token template-string string">Failed after </span><span class="token template-string interpolation interpolation-punctuation punctuation">${</span><span class="token template-string interpolation">maxIterations</span><span class="token template-string interpolation interpolation-punctuation punctuation">}</span><span class="token template-string string"> iterations. Last error: </span><span class="token template-string interpolation interpolation-punctuation punctuation">${</span><span class="token template-string interpolation">lastError</span><span class="token template-string interpolation interpolation-punctuation punctuation">}</span><span class="token template-string template-punctuation string">`</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L41" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> iterations</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token plain"> maxIterations</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L42" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L43" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token punctuation">}</span></div></div></code></pre></div></div><p class="text-pretty break-words">The <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">&quot;use workflow&quot;</code> directive marks this function as durable. If the process crashes or redeploys mid-run, it replays from the last completed step rather than starting over. The <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">while</code> loop creates the self-healing behavior: when tests fail, the next iteration includes the error message so the model can correct its approach.</p><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#4.-define-the-steps" id="4.-define-the-steps"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>4. Define the steps</h2><p class="text-pretty break-words">Each step is an async function with the <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">&quot;use step&quot;</code> directive. Steps get automatic retries on failure, run as separate requests, and their inputs and outputs are recorded for observability.</p><h3 class="text-heading-20 leading-[1.1] font-[450]! @lg:text-heading-32 @lg:text-[28px]! [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#generate-code" id="generate-code"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Generate code</h3><p class="text-pretty break-words">This step sends the coding task to Claude via AI Gateway. On retry iterations, it includes the previous error so the model can adjust its approach.</p><div data-kb-copy-code="async function generateCode(prompt: string, previousError: string | null) {

&quot;use step&quot;;


const systemPrompt = `You are a code generator. Write a single TypeScript file that solves the given task. Export the main function as a named export. Only output the code, no explanation.${

previousError

? `\n\nYour previous attempt failed with this error:\n${previousError}\nFix the issue.`

: &quot;&quot;

}`;


const { text } = await generateText({

model: gateway(&quot;anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6&quot;),

system: systemPrompt,

prompt,

});


return stripCodeFences(text);

});


return stripCodeFences(text);

</span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L10" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword">return</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function">stripCodeFences</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token plain">text</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L11" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token punctuation">}</span></div></div></code></pre></div></div><h3 class="text-heading-20 leading-[1.1] font-[450]! @lg:text-heading-32 @lg:text-[28px]! [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#execute-in-sandbox" id="execute-in-sandbox"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Execute in Sandbox</h3><p class="text-pretty break-words">Sandbox spins up an isolated Firecracker microVM, writes the generated code and tests into it, and runs them. The Sandbox is completely isolated, so even if the generated code does something destructive, it can&#x27;t affect your application.</p><div data-kb-copy-code="async function executeInSandbox(code: string, tests: string) {

&quot;use step&quot;;


const sandbox = await Sandbox.create({ runtime: &quot;node24&quot; });


try {

// Write the generated files into the sandbox

await sandbox.writeFiles([

{ path: &quot;solution.ts&quot;, content: Buffer.from(code) },

{ path: &quot;test.ts&quot;, content: Buffer.from(tests) },

]);


// Install tsx so we can run TypeScript directly

await sandbox.runCommand(&quot;npm&quot;, [&quot;install&quot;, &quot;-g&quot;, &quot;tsx&quot;]);


// Run the tests

const result = await sandbox.runCommand(&quot;npx&quot;, [&quot;tsx&quot;, &quot;test.ts&quot;]);


const stdout = await result.stdout();

const stderr = await result.stderr();


if (result.exitCode === 0 &amp;&amp; stdout.includes(&quot;ALL TESTS PASSED&quot;)) {

return { passed: true, output: stdout, error: null };

}


return {

passed: false,

output: stdout,

error: stderr || stdout || &quot;Tests failed with no output&quot;,

};

} finally {

await sandbox.stop();

}

off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain" style="display:inline-block">

</span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L26" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword">return</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L27" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword">finally</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C4fde12f9-L32" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; Each Sandbox is ephemeral, so there&#x27;s nothing to persist after capturing the output.</p><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#5.-create-the-api-route" id="5.-create-the-api-route"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>5. Create the API route</h2><p class="text-pretty break-words">The route handler triggers the workflow and returns immediately. The workflow runs asynchronously on Vercel&#x27;s infrastructure, backed by <a href="https://vercel.com/docs/queues" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-zone="null" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-[var(--geist-link-color)] [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:underline [&amp;_u]:no-underline underline decoration-1 underline-offset-2 text-inherit! hover:decoration-1! hover:text-(--geist-link-color)! focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2">Vercel Queues</a>.</p><div data-kb-copy-code="import { start } from &quot;workflow/api&quot;;

import { evaluateCode } from &quot;@/workflows/evaluate-code&quot;;

import { NextResponse } from &quot;next/server&quot;;


export async function POST(request: Request) {

const { prompt } = await request.json();


await start(evaluateCode, [prompt]);


return NextResponse.json({

message: &quot;Evaluation workflow started&quot;,

});

class="text-pretty break-words">Back in the terminal where your dev server is running, you&#x27;ll see a series of <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">POST</code> requests to <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">/.well-known/workflow/v1/flow</code> and <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">/.well-known/workflow/v1/step</code>. The <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">/step</code> requests are your individual step functions executing (LLM calls and sandbox execution), and the <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">/flow</code> requests are the orchestrator progressing the workflow state between steps. The step requests taking 2-6 seconds are typically LLM calls through AI Gateway, while shorter ones are the orchestrator advancing to the next step.</p><p class="text-pretty break-words">These logs confirm the workflow is running, but they don&#x27;t show you the result. text-[var(--ds-gray-1000)]" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off" data-geist-code-block="true"><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C3ba84ea7-L1" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C3ba84ea7-L2" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain"> </span><span http://localhost:3000/api/evaluate</span></div></div></code></pre></div></div><p class="text-pretty break-words">This prompt is more likely to trigger the retry loop. The model might produce a subtly incorrect implementation on the first pass, the tests catch it, and the workflow loops back with the error context. You&#x27;ll see more <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">/step</code> and <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">/flow</code> requests in your terminal as the workflow iterates. That self-healing cycle is the core value of combining durable orchestration with safe execution.</p><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#7.-inspect-runs-locally" id="7.-inspect-runs-locally"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>7. text-[var(--ds-gray-1000)]" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off" data-geist-code-block="true"><div class="line" data-geist-code-block-line="true" id="C162d343f-L1" data-highlighted="false" style="font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; off"><div class="token-line"><span class="token plain">npx workflow inspect runs </span><span class="token parameter variable">--web</span></div></div></code></pre></div></div><p class="text-pretty break-words">This opens a browser-based inspector that reads from <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">.next/workflow-data/</code> in your project directory. You can see each run, its steps, inputs, outputs, and timing without deploying anything.</p><p class="text-pretty break-words">You can also inspect Sandbox activity during local development. Open the <a href="https://vercel.com/dashboard" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-zone="null" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-[var(--geist-link-color)] [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:underline [&amp;_u]:no-underline underline decoration-1 underline-offset-2 text-inherit! hover:decoration-1! hover:text-(--geist-link-color)! focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2">Vercel Dashboard</a>, select your project, and navigate to <span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Sandboxes</span> in the left sidebar. Sandbox invocations from localhost are visible here because they authenticate through Vercel&#x27;s OIDC tokens.</p><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#8.-observe-in-the-dashboard-after-deploying" id="8.-observe-in-the-dashboard-after-deploying"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>8. Observe in the dashboard after deploying</h2><p class="text-pretty break-words">Deploy the app to see the full observability picture. Workflow runs are only recorded in the Vercel Dashboard for deployed applications, not from localhost.</p><p class="text-pretty break-words">Once deployed, open your <a href="https://vercel.com/dashboard" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-zone="null" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-[var(--geist-link-color)] [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:underline [&amp;_u]:no-underline underline decoration-1 underline-offset-2 text-inherit! hover:decoration-1! hover:text-(--geist-link-color)! focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2">Vercel Dashboard</a>, select your project, and check three areas:</p><p class="text-pretty break-words"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Workflows</span> (left sidebar → <span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Workflows</span>): Each run is listed with every step, its inputs and outputs, timing, and status. If a retry happened, you can trace what failed and what the model did differently on the next attempt. No logging code is required for this; it&#x27;s automatic.</p><p class="text-pretty break-words"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Sandboxes</span> (left sidebar → <span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Sandboxes</span>): Every Sandbox invocation is logged with its creation time, runtime, and duration. You can verify that sandboxes are being created and stopped correctly for each execution step.</p><p class="text-pretty break-words"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">AI Gateway</span> (left sidebar → <span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">AI Gateway</span>): Every LLM call shows the model used, token count, latency, and cost. This helps you understand how much each evaluation costs and whether a faster or cheaper model would work for certain steps.</p><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#best-practices" id="best-practices"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Best practices</h2><h3 class="text-heading-20 leading-[1.1] font-[450]! @lg:text-heading-32 @lg:text-[28px]! [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#strip-markdown-code-fences-from-llm-output" id="strip-markdown-code-fences-from-llm-output"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Strip markdown code fences from LLM output</h3><p class="text-pretty break-words">LLMs frequently wrap code in markdown fences (<code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">```typescript ... ```</code>) even when instructed not to. If those fences get written to the Sandbox as-is, the runtime tries to execute them as code and throws a <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">TypeError</code>. The <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">stripCodeFences</code> helper in step 3 handles this, but it&#x27;s worth remembering whenever you write LLM output to a file system.</p><h3 class="text-heading-20 leading-[1.1] font-[450]! @lg:text-heading-32 @lg:text-[28px]! [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#always-clean-up-sandbox-instances" id="always-clean-up-sandbox-instances"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Always clean up Sandbox instances</h3><p class="text-pretty break-words">Use a <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">finally</code> block to call <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">sandbox.stop()</code>. Sandboxes have a default 5-minute timeout, but stopping them explicitly keeps costs down and avoids hitting concurrency limits.</p><h3 class="text-heading-20 leading-[1.1] font-[450]! @lg:text-heading-32 @lg:text-[28px]! [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#use-fatalerror-for-non-retryable-failures" id="use-fatalerror-for-non-retryable-failures"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Use <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">FatalError</code> for non-retryable failures</h3><p class="text-pretty break-words">By default, steps retry on any thrown error. If you want a step to fail permanently (for example, invalid user input), throw a <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">FatalError</code> from the <code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">workflow</code> package instead.</p><h3 class="text-heading-20 leading-[1.1] font-[450]! @lg:text-heading-32 @lg:text-[28px]! [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#snapshot-repeated-sandbox-setups" id="snapshot-repeated-sandbox-setups"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Snapshot repeated Sandbox setups</h3><p class="text-pretty break-words">If every run installs the same dependencies, <a href="https://vercel.com/docs/vercel-sandbox/concepts/snapshots" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-zone="null" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-[var(--geist-link-color)] [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:underline [&amp;_u]:no-underline underline decoration-1 underline-offset-2 text-inherit! hover:decoration-1! hover:text-(--geist-link-color)! focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2">snapshot</a> a Sandbox after the install step and create new instances from that snapshot. This saves setup time on every subsequent run.</p><h3 class="text-heading-20 leading-[1.1] font-[450]! @lg:text-heading-32 @lg:text-[28px]! [&amp;:has(_b)]:text-gray-900 [&amp;_b]:text-gray-1000 [&amp;_b]:font-normal group relative w-full text-balance pt-[calc(var(--grid-divider-gap)/1.5)]"><a class="absolute flex h-[1lh] w-8 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-900 opacity-0 transition-all duration-100 -translate-x-full group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-gray-1000 focus-visible:opacity-100 scroll-mt-10" href="#use-different-models-for-different-steps" id="use-different-models-for-different-steps"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Use different models for different steps</h3><p class="text-pretty break-words">AI Gateway makes it straightforward to assign different models to different steps. You might use a faster, cheaper model for test generation and a more capable one for code generation, each specified independently.</p><h2 class="text-heading-24 font-[450]! @md:text-heading-32 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg><span class="sr-only">Copy link to heading</span></a>Next steps</h2><ul class="my-3 list-disc pl-6
